  <div class="ff-privacy-hero">
    <span class="label">FicForge Legal</span>
    <h1>Privacy Policy</h1>
    <p>
      This Privacy Policy explains how FicForge may collect, use, store, protect, and manage information connected to accounts, stories, forums, contact forms, Premium memberships, Sparks, analytics, and platform activity.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-privacy-note">
    <strong>Important note</strong>
    <p>
      This page is a working Privacy Policy  for FicForge. It will be reviewed and updated as the platform grows.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-privacy-panel">
    <h2>1. Information FicForge may collect</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge may collect information that users provide directly, information created through platform activity, and technical information needed to operate and protect the site.
    </p>
    <ul>
      <li>Account information, such as username, display name, email address, password credentials, and profile details.</li>
      <li>Creator information, such as story titles, chapters, descriptions, covers, tags, ratings, warnings, profile text, and creator settings.</li>
      <li>Community information, such as comments, reviews, forum posts, reports, appeals, messages, and moderation history.</li>
      <li>Support information, such as contact form submissions, screenshots, issue descriptions, account questions, and support replies.</li>
      <li>Payment-related information connected to Premium memberships, Sparks, creator support, refunds, payout reviews, and transaction records.</li>
      <li>Technical information, such as IP address, browser type, device information, login activity, cookies, site activity, security logs, and analytics data.</li>
    </ul>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-privacy-panel">
    <h2>2. How FicForge uses information</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge uses information to operate the platform, provide user accounts, support publishing, manage community features, improve the site, process support requests, and protect the platform from abuse.
    </p>
    <ul>
      <li>To create and manage user accounts.</li>
      <li>To display stories, chapters, creator profiles, comments, reviews, forums, and public activity.</li>
      <li>To provide browsing, writing, publishing, community, Premium, Sparks, and support features.</li>
      <li>To respond to contact requests, reports, appeals, DMCA notices, payment questions, and account issues.</li>
      <li>To review moderation concerns, policy violations, suspicious activity, fraud, spam, and security risks.</li>
      <li>To improve FicForge’s performance, layout, tools, content discovery, reader experience, and creator systems.</li>
    </ul>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-privacy-panel">
    <h2>3. Public content and profile visibility</h2>
    <p>
      Some information users submit to FicForge may be public or visible to other users.
    </p>
    <ul>
      <li>Published stories, chapters, covers, tags, ratings, warnings, summaries, and author notes may be publicly visible.</li>
      <li>Creator profiles, display names, badges, public support status, and public profile text may be visible to readers.</li>
      <li>Forum posts, comments, reviews, recommendations, and community activity may be visible depending on platform settings.</li>
      <li>Users should not post private personal information they do not want visible to others.</li>
    </ul>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-privacy-panel">
    <h2>4. Payments, Premium, Sparks, and creator payouts</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge may use third-party payment providers to process payments for Premium memberships, Sparks, creator support, and future monetization features.
    </p>
    <p>
      FicForge may store limited transaction-related records needed to manage purchases, support requests, refunds, chargebacks, fraud prevention, account reviews, platform fees, creator balances, payout eligibility, and tax or compliance requirements.
    </p>
    <p>
      Payment providers may collect and process payment details under their own privacy policies and terms. FicForge should not store full payment card details directly unless a future payment system requires it and proper security measures are in place.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-privacy-panel">
    <h2>5. Contact forms and support requests</h2>
    <p>
      When users contact FicForge, submit reports, send appeals, file copyright concerns, or ask for support, FicForge may collect the information included in those forms.
    </p>
    <ul>
      <li>Name or display name.</li>
      <li>Email address.</li>
      <li>FicForge username.</li>
      <li>Support type.</li>
      <li>Relevant story, profile, forum, or page links.</li>
      <li>Message details, screenshots, attachments, and issue descriptions.</li>
    </ul>
    <p>
      FicForge uses this information to investigate, respond, resolve issues, enforce rules, and maintain support records.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-privacy-panel">
    <h2>6. Cookies and similar technologies</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge may use cookies and similar technologies to keep users signed in, remember preferences, protect accounts, improve performance, support analytics, manage forums, and operate platform features.
    </p>
    <p>
      Cookies may also be used by WordPress, Elementor, forum tools, analytics tools, security tools, payment providers, embedded content, or future advertising systems.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-privacy-panel">
    <h2>7. Analytics and site improvement</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge may use analytics tools to understand site traffic, page performance, reader activity, user engagement, and platform growth.
    </p>
    <p>
      Analytics may include information such as visited pages, referral sources, device type, browser type, approximate location, session activity, and technical performance data. FicForge uses this information to improve the site and make better platform decisions.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-privacy-panel">
    <h2>8. Advertising and ad-free memberships</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge may introduce advertising in the future. If advertising is used, ads may involve third-party advertising partners, sponsored placements, or platform-managed promotional spaces.
    </p>
    <p>
      Premium members receive an ad-free experience according to FicForge’s membership benefits. Advertising systems, if added, should be configured to respect Premium ad-free access where applicable.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-privacy-panel">
    <h2>9. Sharing information</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge may share information only when needed to operate the platform, provide services, comply with legal obligations, protect users, or work with trusted service providers.
    </p>
    <ul>
      <li>Hosting providers that keep FicForge online.</li>
      <li>Email providers used for contact, support, notices, and account communication.</li>
      <li>Payment providers used for purchases, refunds, payouts, and transaction review.</li>
      <li>Analytics, security, anti-spam, forum, membership, or site-management tools.</li>
      <li>Legal, copyright, safety, or compliance processes when required or appropriate.</li>
    </ul>
    <p>
      FicForge does not intend to sell personal information as a business model.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-privacy-panel">
    <h2>10. User choices and account control</h2>
    <p>
      Users may be able to update account details, profile information, display names, email addresses, passwords, notification preferences, and public profile content through their account settings.
    </p>
    <p>
      Users may also contact FicForge for support with account access, privacy questions, deletion requests, corrections, or other data-related concerns.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-privacy-panel">
    <h2>11. Data retention</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge may keep information for as long as needed to operate accounts, display content, manage creator tools, process transactions, handle support requests, investigate reports, comply with legal obligations, prevent abuse, and maintain platform records.
    </p>
    <p>
      Some information may remain in backups, logs, moderation records, transaction records, legal records, or security systems for a period of time after content or accounts are changed or removed.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-privacy-panel">
    <h2>12. Security</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge uses reasonable efforts to protect user information and platform systems. However, no website, hosting system, payment system, database, or online service can guarantee perfect security.
    </p>
    <p>
      Users should use strong passwords, protect their login information, and report suspicious activity through Contact Support.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-privacy-panel">
    <h2>13. Children and younger users</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge is intended for users who are old enough to use online accounts, community features, and platform services under applicable rules. Users who are not old enough to use FicForge should not create accounts or submit personal information without appropriate permission.
    </p>
    <p>
      FicForge may restrict, review, or remove accounts or content where age, safety, or legal concerns apply.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-privacy-panel">
    <h2>14. International users</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge may be accessed by users in different countries. By using FicForge, users understand that information may be processed through hosting, email, payment, analytics, security, and platform tools that may operate in different locations.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-privacy-panel">
    <h2>15. Changes to this Privacy Policy</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge may update this Privacy Policy as the platform changes, new features launch, laws change, providers change, or new privacy practices become necessary.
    </p>
    <p>
      Updated versions may be posted on this page. Continued use of FicForge after updates means users accept the updated policy.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-privacy-panel">
    <h2>16. Contact</h2>
    <p>
      Questions about this Privacy Policy, account data, privacy concerns, or data-related requests can be sent through FicForge’s Contact Support page.
    </p>

    <div class="ff-privacy-links">
      <a href="/contact/">Contact Support</a>
      <a href="/terms/">Terms of Service</a>
      <a href="/content-policy/">Content Policy</a>
      <a href="/dmca-copyright/">DMCA & Copyright</a>
      <a href="/support/">Support Hub</a>
    </div>

  <div class="ff-privacy-bottom">
    <h2>FicForge Privacy Policy</h2>
    <p>
      This Privacy Policy is part of FicForge’s platform foundation and should continue to be updated as accounts, forums, payments, Premium memberships, Sparks, creator support, advertising, and platform tools become more advanced.
    </p>
  </div>

PAGE: Protection & Rights  (slug: protection-rights)
================================================================================

<section class="ff-rights-page">
  <div class="ff-rights-hero">
    <span>CREATOR PROTECTION</span>
    <h1>Protection & Rights</h1>
    <p>
      FicForge is built to give writers a clear, respectful place to publish stories while understanding how ownership, fanfiction, original webnovels, copyright, monetization, and platform protections work.
    </p>

    <div class="ff-rights-actions">
      <a href="/support/">← Return to Support</a>
      <a class="primary" href="/dmca-copyright/">DMCA & Copyright</a>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-rights-grid">
    <div class="ff-rights-card">
      <strong>Original Fiction Ownership</strong>
      <p>
        Writers keep ownership of their original stories, characters, worlds, and intellectual property. Publishing on FicForge does not transfer ownership of your work to the platform.
      </p>
    </div>

    <div class="ff-rights-card">
      <strong>Fanfiction & Transformative Works</strong>
      <p>
        Fanfiction can be published and shared on FicForge, but fanfiction chapters cannot be sold directly, locked behind chapter paywalls, or monetized as copyrighted derivative works.
      </p>
    </div>

    <div class="ff-rights-card">
      <strong>Anti-Scraping Position</strong>
      <p>
        FicForge does not support scraping, reposting, unauthorized compilation, or mass collection of creator work. Abuse may lead to account restrictions or permanent removal.
      </p>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-rights-panel">
    <span>CREATOR OWNERSHIP</span>
    <h2>Your stories remain yours</h2>
    <p>
      When you publish original fiction on FicForge, you remain the owner of your original intellectual property. FicForge does not claim ownership over your characters, worlds, plots, prose, or future publishing opportunities.
    </p>
    <p>
      By posting on the platform, you give FicForge limited permission to host, display, organize, cache, and promote your work inside normal platform features. This permission exists so your story can appear on your profile, in search results, in story listings, in reader libraries, and other FicForge discovery tools.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-rights-panel">
    <span>FANFICTION BOUNDARIES</span>
    <h2>Fanfiction is free to read and separate from direct sales</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge welcomes fanfiction and transformative storytelling, but fanfiction chapters cannot be sold directly, locked behind Sparks, placed behind Fast Pass, offered as paid early access, or tied to paid chapter continuations.
    </p>
    <p>
      FicForge’s safe  support model separates financial support from specific fanfiction works. Readers may support a creator’s overall public profile as general appreciation for that creator’s time, talent, and broader creative presence, but that support is not a purchase of any specific fanfiction story, chapter, fandom, ship, character, update, or continuation.
    </p>
    <p>
      Creators may not promise bonus fanfiction, exclusive fanfiction, early fanfiction access, fandom-specific rewards, or future fanfiction updates in exchange for Sparks, subscriptions, tips, donations, or outside payments.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-rights-panel">
    <span>GENERAL CREATOR SUPPORT</span>
    <h2>Profile support is not story payment</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge may allow readers to support a creator’s public profile as a broad show of appreciation. This support is directed toward the creator as an independent creative person, not toward the sale of any specific fanfiction work.
    </p>
    <p>
      This means profile support should not be advertised as payment for a particular fanfiction chapter, fandom project, ship, character, continuation, bonus scene, commission, or release schedule.The profile support is there as a show of respect and solidarity as   writers  invested their sweat, time and tears for a creative work they wish to share with others for free.
    </p>
    <p>
      FicForge’s goal is to maintain a clear separation between general creator appreciation and the direct sale of unlicensed derivative works. This approach reduces legal risk, and FicForge may restrict or disable support tools if a creator uses them to indirectly sell fanfiction or promise fanfiction rewards.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-rights-panel">
    <span>ORIGINAL WEBNOVEL RIGHTS</span>
    <h2>Original webnovels can use full creator monetization tools</h2>
    <p>
      Original webnovels are treated differently from fanfiction because the creator owns the underlying original world, characters, story structure, and intellectual property. This gives original fiction authors access to broader publishing and monetization options on FicForge.
    </p>
    <p>
      Original fiction creators may later use FicForge monetization tools such as Sparks-based Fast Pass, creator subscriptions, gated early-access chapters, and other approved earning systems. These tools are designed for stories where the creator controls the original rights.
    </p>
    <p>
      FicForge’s goal is to let original webnovel authors build sustainable audiences without surrendering massive ownership of their work. Publishing an original story on FicForge does not transfer copyright ownership to the platform.
    </p>
    <p>
      Original webnovel creators remain responsible for making sure their work is truly original, properly tagged, correctly rated, and compliant with FicForge’s content and payment rules.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-rights-panel">
    <span>ORIGINAL FICTION MONETIZATION</span>
    <h2>How original creators may earn on FicForge</h2>
    <p>
      Original fiction and original webnovels may eventually use FicForge’s creator earning systems because the creator owns the core work being monetized. This can include Sparks-based early access, Fast Pass chapters, creator subscriptions, and approved profile support.
    </p>
    <p>
      Fast Pass is intended for original webnovels only. It allows impatient readers to spend Sparks to access selected upcoming chapters early, while patient readers can still wait for scheduled free release.
    </p>
    <p>
      Creator subscriptions may later allow original authors to offer broader supporter benefits, early access collections, behind-the-scenes material, or other original-content rewards, as long as those rewards follow FicForge’s content and payment rules.
    </p>
    <p>
      FicForge may apply platform revenue sharing, payout thresholds, verification requirements, and payment processor rules to monetized original fiction. These systems are meant to support creators while keeping the platform sustainable.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-rights-panel">
    <span>PLATFORM PROTECTION</span>
    <h2>What FicForge can help with</h2>

    <div class="ff-rights-list">
      <div>
        <strong>Plagiarism Reports</strong>
        <p>Creators and readers may report stolen, copied, or improperly reposted work for review.</p>
      </div>

      <div>
        <strong>Copyright Concerns</strong>
        <p>Rights holders can submit copyright concerns through the DMCA & Copyright process.</p>
      </div>

      <div>
        <strong>Impersonation Reports</strong>
        <p>Users can report accounts pretending to be another creator, brand, or public identity.</p>
      </div>

      <div>
        <strong>Scraping Abuse</strong>
        <p>Accounts or systems involved in unauthorized collection, reposting, or compilation may be restricted.</p>
      </div>

      <div>
        <strong>Content Misuse</strong>
        <p>FicForge may investigate cases where creator work is being misused, spammed, duplicated, or misrepresented on the platform.</p>
      </div>

      <div>
        <strong>Policy Enforcement</strong>
        <p>Violations of FicForge rules may lead to warnings, content restriction, account suspension, or permanent removal.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-rights-panel">
    <span>IMPORTANT LIMITATIONS</span>
    <h2>No platform can promise perfect protection</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge can use platform rules, reporting systems, takedown support, moderation, and technical safeguards to discourage abuse. However, no public website can fully prevent screenshots, copying, manual reposting, scraping attempts, or misuse by bad actors. But ofcourse we'll use 110% of our efforts to deter and stop any such action against our community.
    </p>
    <p>
      FicForge’s role is to provide reasonable platform protections, clear rules, and a process for responding to abuse when it is reported.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-rights-panel">
    <span>DMCA & COPYRIGHT</span>
    <h2>Copyright holders and creators have a reporting path</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge respects intellectual property rights. Copyright holders may submit takedown notices, and creators may submit counter-notices if they believe a removal was made in error.
    </p>
    <p>
      Repeated copyright violations, plagiarism, malicious reposting, or abuse of other creators’ work may result in monetization restrictions, content removal, account suspension, or permanent termination.
    </p>

    <div class="ff-rights-actions lower">
      <a class="primary" href="/dmca-copyright/">Read DMCA & Copyright Policy</a>
      <a href="/contact/">Contact Support</a>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-rights-warning">
    <strong>Important note</strong>
    <p>
      This page explains FicForge platform rules and creator support practices. It is not legal advice. FicForge cannot guarantee that every use of fanfiction will be protected from copyright claims, takedown requests, or rights-holder objections. Creators with serious legal, publishing, contract, copyright, or intellectual property concerns should seek qualified legal guidance.
    </p>
  </div>
</section>

  <div class="ff-sparks-hero">
    <span class="label">Sparks System</span>
    <h1>Sparks FAQ</h1>
    <p>
      Sparks are FicForge’s platform currency for supporting creators, powering approved platform features, and giving readers a simple way to participate in the FicForge creator economy.
    </p>

    <div class="ff-sparks-actions">
      <a href="/support/">← Return to Support</a>
      <a class="primary" href="/premium/">View Premium</a>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-sparks-alert">
    <strong>Important launch note</strong>
    <p>
      Sparks are part of FicForge’s planned monetization system. Some purchasing, wallet, support, and creator-earning features may launch in stages as the platform grows.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-sparks-pricing">
    <div class="ff-sparks-card price">
      <span class="tag">Starter</span>
      <strong>100 Sparks</strong>
      <div class="ff-sparks-price">$1</div>
      <p>A small pack for testing Sparks or giving light creator support.</p>
    </div>

    <div class="ff-sparks-card price">
      <span class="tag">Standard</span>
      <strong>1,000 Sparks</strong>
      <div class="ff-sparks-price">$10</div>
      <p>A simple everyday pack for casual readers and regular supporters.</p>
    </div>

    <div class="ff-sparks-card price">
      <span class="tag">Supporter</span>
      <strong>5,000 Sparks</strong>
      <div class="ff-sparks-price">$50</div>
      <p>For active readers who support creators more often.</p>
      <div class="ff-sparks-bonus">+500 bonus Sparks</div>
    </div>

    <div class="ff-sparks-card price">
      <span class="tag">Power Reader</span>
      <strong>8,000 Sparks</strong>
      <div class="ff-sparks-price">$80</div>
      <p>For frequent readers who want a larger Spark balance.</p>
      <div class="ff-sparks-bonus">+1,200 bonus Sparks</div>
    </div>

    <div class="ff-sparks-card price best">
      <span class="tag">Best Value</span>
      <strong>15,000 Sparks</strong>
      <div class="ff-sparks-price">$125</div>
      <p>The premium Spark package for heavy supporters and active readers.</p>
      <div class="ff-sparks-bonus">+2,500 bonus Sparks</div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-sparks-panel">
    <span class="label">Basics</span>
    <h2>What are Sparks?</h2>
    <p>
      Sparks are a FicForge platform currency that readers may use through approved FicForge features. They make it easier to support creators without making a separate payment every time.
    </p>

    <div class="ff-sparks-two">
      <div class="ff-sparks-box">
        <strong>For readers</strong>
        <p>
          Sparks let readers support creators, participate in future platform features, and manage support from one FicForge wallet.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div class="ff-sparks-box">
        <strong>For creators</strong>
        <p>
          Sparks may contribute to eligible creator earnings when used through approved support features and when the creator account meets payout requirements.
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-sparks-panel">
    <span class="label">Spark Packs</span>
    <h2>How much do Sparks cost?</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge uses simple Spark packs so readers can choose how much they want to add to their account.
    </p>

    <div class="ff-sparks-highlight">
      <p>
        <strong>Starter:</strong> 100 Sparks — $1<br>
        <strong>Standard:</strong> 1,000 Sparks — $10<br>
        <strong>Supporter:</strong> 5,000 Sparks + 500 bonus — $50<br>
        <strong>Power Reader:</strong> 8,000 Sparks + 1,200 bonus — $80<br>
        <strong>Patron:</strong> 15,000 Sparks + 2,500 bonus — $125
      </p>
    </div>

    <p style="margin-top:14px;">
      Bonus Sparks are promotional extras included with larger packs. Spark prices may account for platform operations, payment processing, fraud prevention, refunds, taxes, and service costs.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-sparks-panel">
    <span class="label">Using Sparks</span>
    <h2>What can Sparks be used for?</h2>
    <p>
      Sparks may be used only through approved FicForge features. The exact features may expand over time as the platform develops.
    </p>

    <div class="ff-sparks-list">
      <div>
        <strong>General creator support</strong>
        <p>Readers may use Sparks to support a creator’s public profile as general appreciation for their presence and writing activity on FicForge.</p>
      </div>

      <div>
        <strong>Original webnovel features</strong>
        <p>Original webnovels may become eligible for approved monetization features as FicForge expands its creator tools.</p>
      </div>

      <div>
        <strong>Future platform features</strong>
        <p>Sparks may later support approved features such as event participation, reader boosts, creator tools, profile upgrades, or other FicForge-native features.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-sparks-panel">
    <span class="label">Limits</span>
    <h2>What Sparks cannot buy</h2>
    <p>
      Sparks are not a way to bypass FicForge’s content, copyright, fanfiction, or payment rules.
    </p>

    <div class="ff-sparks-list">
      <div>
        <strong>No paid fanfiction chapters</strong>
        <p>Sparks cannot be used to buy, unlock, Fast Pass, request, commission, or receive paid early access to fanfiction chapters.</p>
      </div>

      <div>
        <strong>No specific fanfiction rewards</strong>
        <p>Sparks cannot be exchanged for a specific fanfiction story, chapter, fandom, update, continuation, ship, character, bonus scene, private scene, or reward.</p>
      </div>

      <div>
        <strong>No rule-breaking content</strong>
        <p>Sparks cannot be used to purchase, encourage, or reward content that violates FicForge’s Content Policy, copyright rules, payment rules, or safety rules.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-sparks-panel">
    <span class="label">Fanfiction Boundary</span>
    <h2>How Sparks work around fanfiction</h2>
    <p>
      Fanfiction chapters on FicForge remain free to read. They cannot be sold, locked behind payment, placed behind Fast Pass, offered as paid early access, or used as paid rewards.
    </p>

    <div class="ff-sparks-list">
      <div>
        <strong>Allowed</strong>
        <p>General profile support for a creator’s overall public presence on FicForge, as long as it is not tied to any specific fanfiction work or reward.</p>
      </div>

      <div>
        <strong>Not allowed</strong>
        <p>Promising fanfiction chapters, continuations, bonus scenes, fandom requests, private updates, commissions, or special rewards in exchange for Sparks or payment.</p>
      </div>

      <div>
        <strong>Why this rule exists</strong>
        <p>This boundary helps FicForge separate general creator support from selling or monetizing third-party fanfiction content.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-sparks-panel">
    <span class="label">Creator Conversion</span>
    <h2>Do creators receive the same amount readers pay?</h2>
    <p>
      No. Reader purchase pricing and creator-side earnings are related, but they are not the same thing.
    </p>

    <div class="ff-sparks-highlight">
      <p>
        <strong>Reader purchase example:</strong> 1,000 Sparks costs $10<br>
        <strong>Creator-side conversion:</strong> 100 eligible Sparks = $1.00<br>
        <strong>Creator payout threshold:</strong> 5,000 eligible Sparks = $50.00
      </p>
    </div>

    <p style="margin-top:14px;">
      For payout rules, payout timing, holds, account review, payment methods, and creator tax responsibility, visit the Creator Payout FAQ.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-sparks-panel">
    <span class="label">Refunds & Reversals</span>
    <h2>Can Sparks be refunded?</h2>
    <p>
      Refunds may depend on payment status, payment processor rules, platform review, account activity, fraud concerns, and whether Sparks have already been used.
    </p>

    <div class="ff-sparks-list">
      <div>
        <strong>Unused Sparks</strong>
        <p>Refund requests for unused Sparks may be reviewed by FicForge according to platform policy and payment processor rules.</p>
      </div>

      <div>
        <strong>Used Sparks</strong>
        <p>Sparks that have already been used for support or platform features may not always be refundable.</p>
      </div>

      <div>
        <strong>Chargebacks and disputes</strong>
        <p>Payment disputes, chargebacks, refunds, or reversals may affect account balances, creator earnings, and payout eligibility.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-sparks-panel">
    <span class="label">Safety</span>
    <h2>Abuse prevention and account review</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge may review Spark activity to protect readers, creators, and the platform from fraud, abuse, manipulation, copyright issues, and payment misuse.
    </p>

    <div class="ff-sparks-list">
      <div>
        <strong>Suspicious activity</strong>
        <p>Unusual purchases, fake support patterns, refund abuse, chargebacks, stolen payment methods, or coordinated abuse may trigger review.</p>
      </div>

      <div>
        <strong>Creator account review</strong>
        <p>Creator earnings connected to suspicious Spark activity may be delayed, held, reversed, or removed from eligible payout balance.</p>
      </div>

      <div>
        <strong>Platform action</strong>
        <p>FicForge may restrict wallets, pause payouts, remove access to monetized tools, or suspend accounts when Spark abuse is found.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-sparks-panel">
    <span class="label">Related Pages</span>
    <h2>Where to go next</h2>
    <p>
      This page explains Sparks from the reader and platform-currency side. Use the pages below for deeper rules.
    </p>

    <div class="ff-sparks-links">
      <a href="/creator-payout-faq/">Creator Payout FAQ</a>
      <a href="/premium/">Premium</a>
      <a href="/protection-rights/">Protection & Rights</a>
      <a href="/content-policy/">Content Policy</a>
      <a href="/dmca-copyright/">DMCA & Copyright</a>
      <a href="/contact/">Contact Support</a>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-sparks-bottom">
    <h2>Need help with Sparks?</h2>
    <p>
      Contact FicForge Support if you have a question about Spark purchases, wallet balance, creator support, refunds, or suspicious activity.
    </p>

    <div class="ff-sparks-actions">
      <a class="primary" href="/contact/">Contact Support</a>
      <a href="/support/">Return to Support</a>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

  <div class="ff-terms-hero">
    <span class="label">FicForge Legal</span>
    <h1>Terms of Service</h1>
    <p>
      These Terms explain the basic rules for using FicForge, including accounts, publishing, community activity, Premium memberships, Sparks, creator support, and platform boundaries.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-terms-note">
    <strong>Important note</strong>
    <p>
      This page is a working Terms of Service for FicForge. It will be reviewed and updated as the platform grows.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-terms-panel">
    <h2>1. Acceptance of these Terms</h2>
    <p>
      By accessing or using FicForge, you agree to follow these Terms, our Content Policy, Privacy Policy, DMCA & Copyright Policy, community rules, and any other platform rules that apply to your use of the service.
    </p>
    <p>
      If you do not agree with these Terms, you should not use FicForge.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-terms-panel">
    <h2>2. What FicForge is</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge is a fiction platform for readers, fanfiction writers, original fiction authors, serialized storytelling, creator profiles, community discussion, and approved creator-support features.
    </p>
    <p>
      FicForge is built around stories first. Community features, Premium memberships, Sparks, forums, support tools, and creator systems exist to support the reading and writing experience.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-terms-panel">
    <h2>3. Accounts and user responsibility</h2>
    <p>
      You may need an account to publish stories, comment, participate in forums, use Premium features, purchase Sparks, or access creator tools.
    </p>
    <ul>
      <li>You are responsible for keeping your login information secure.</li>
      <li>You may not impersonate another person, creator, company, moderator, or rights-holder.</li>
      <li>You may not use FicForge for spam, fraud, abuse, harassment, or illegal activity.</li>
      <li>FicForge may limit, suspend, or remove accounts that violate platform rules.</li>
    </ul>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-terms-panel">
    <h2>4. User content</h2>
    <p>
      Users may publish or submit stories, chapters, titles, descriptions, tags, covers, profile text, comments, forum posts, reviews, messages, reports, and other content through FicForge.
    </p>
    <p>
      You are responsible for the content you post. By submitting content to FicForge, you confirm that you have the rights or permission needed to share it, or that your use is otherwise allowed under applicable rules.
    </p>
    <p>
      FicForge may remove, restrict, label, moderate, or review content that violates platform rules, creates legal risk, receives credible reports, or appears unsafe for the platform.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-terms-panel">
    <h2>5. Creator ownership and platform license</h2>
    <p>
      Creators keep ownership of the original content they create, subject to any rights they do not own. By posting content on FicForge, you give FicForge permission to host, display, store, format, distribute, promote, and make your content available through the platform.
    </p>
    <p>
      This permission is necessary for FicForge to operate the website, show your stories to readers, display your profile, support browsing, show search results, and run platform features.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-terms-panel">
    <h2>6. Fanfiction boundaries</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge welcomes fanfiction, but fanfiction has different boundaries from original fiction.
    </p>
    <ul>
      <li>Fanfiction chapters remain free to read on FicForge.</li>
      <li>Fanfiction chapters cannot be sold, locked, Fast Passed, or offered as paid early access.</li>
      <li>Creators may not offer paid fanfiction rewards, bonus scenes, continuations, fandom-specific updates, or chapter access in exchange for payment.</li>
      <li>General profile support must not be presented as payment for a specific fanfiction work, chapter, fandom, ship, character, update, continuation, or reward.</li>
    </ul>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-terms-panel">
    <h2>7. Original fiction and monetization</h2>
    <p>
      Original fiction may be eligible for broader monetization features as FicForge develops. This may include approved creator support, future original-fiction tools, and other platform systems.
    </p>
    <p>
      FicForge may review stories, accounts, creator activity, payment activity, and monetization eligibility before allowing or continuing access to creator earning features.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-terms-panel">
    <h2>8. Premium memberships</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge Premium memberships may provide benefits such as monthly Sparks, bonus Sparks, ad-free experience, profile/status perks, priority support, member updates, polls, reading tools, and early access to selected platform features.
    </p>
    <p>
      Premium is a platform membership. It is not paid fanfiction access and does not allow users to bypass FicForge content, copyright, payment, or community rules.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-terms-panel">
    <h2>9. Sparks</h2>
    <p>
      Sparks are FicForge’s platform currency. Sparks may be purchased, included with Premium memberships, awarded as bonuses, or made available through approved platform features.
    </p>
    <ul>
      <li>Sparks may only be used through approved FicForge features.</li>
      <li>Purchased Sparks, bonus Sparks, promotional Sparks, and membership Sparks may have different refund, payout, review, and eligibility rules.</li>
      <li>For eligible creator support, 100 eligible Sparks equals $1.00 before FicForge’s 9% platform fee and before payment-provider, refund, tax, account-review, or processing issues.</li>
      <li>FicForge applies a 9% platform fee to eligible creator-support and monetized creator earnings. Creators keep 91% of eligible support value before other applicable deductions or reviews.</li>
    </ul>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-terms-panel">
    <h2>10. Payments, refunds, and payout reviews</h2>
    <p>
      Payments for Premium memberships, Sparks, creator support, and other paid features may be processed through third-party payment providers.
    </p>
    <p>
      FicForge may review transactions for fraud, abuse, refund risk, chargebacks, account issues, policy violations, payment-provider requirements, or legal concerns.
    </p>
    <p>
      Creator payouts may be delayed, adjusted, denied, or reviewed if there are account problems, refund issues, suspicious activity, eligibility concerns, or policy violations.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-terms-panel">
    <h2>11. Community rules</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge users may participate in comments, reviews, forums, discussions, creator spaces, and other community features.
    </p>
    <ul>
      <li>Criticism and disagreement are allowed when done respectfully.</li>
      <li>Harassment, threats, doxxing, impersonation, spam, targeted abuse, and malicious behavior are not allowed.</li>
      <li>Users may not pressure creators into unsafe, illegal, or rule-breaking content.</li>
      <li>Users may not use community areas to sell fanfiction chapters or paid fanfiction rewards.</li>
    </ul>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-terms-panel">
    <h2>12. Copyright, plagiarism, and takedowns</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge respects creator rights and copyright concerns. Users may not upload stolen work, impersonate creators, repost another person’s writing without permission, or falsely claim ownership of content.
    </p>
    <p>
      Copyright complaints should be submitted through the DMCA & Copyright page. FicForge may remove or restrict content while reviewing credible copyright, plagiarism, or rights-holder concerns.
    </p>
  </div>

  <div class="ff-terms-panel">
    <h2>13. Moderation and enforcement</h2>
    <p>
      FicForge may moderate content, restrict features, remove posts, suspend accounts, limit monetization, hide content, issue warnings, or take other action when users violate platform rules or create risk for the platform.
    </p>

    <script>
(function () {
  function moveCreateStoryForm() {
    const slot = document.getElementById("create-story-form-slot");
    const form = document.querySelector(".ff-create-story-form");

    if (slot && form && !slot.contains(form)) {
      slot.appendChild(form);
    }
  }

  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", moveCreateStoryForm);
  setTimeout(moveCreateStoryForm, 500);
  setTimeout(moveCreateStoryForm, 1500);
})();
</script>

    radial-gradient(circle at top right, rgba(249, 115, 22, 0.12), transparent 30%),
    #050B16;

  opacity: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  transition: opacity 0.18s ease;
}

body.ff-transitioning .ff-page-transition-overlay {
  opacity: 1;
}
</style>

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
  const workroomLinks = document.querySelectorAll('a[href="/my-stories/"], a[href="/my-stories"]');

  workroomLinks.forEach(function(link) {
    link.addEventListener("click", function () {
      document.body.classList.add("ff-transitioning");
    });
  });
});
</script>
